# 2007 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** first Safari on a phone · no App Store
**Star:** iPhone Safari · `itt07-iphone` · `sites/iphone/index.html`
**ILS June:** 121,892,559 · 1,373,327,790
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 284
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt07-iphone`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2007/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2007/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2007 — June 121,892,559 · Jan 106,875,138 · 1,373,327,790
2. ★ iPhone Safari — App Store never writes
3. Street View leftover — 29 May
4. Gmail open leftover — 7 Feb
5. Facebook Platform leftover — 24 May
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt07-iphone`

**Room:** `years/2007/sites/iphone/index.html`
**Hook:** `data-ip07- / data-official-verb`
**Nostalgia:** iPhone Safari — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- App Store · Chrome · Android G1 trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt07-iphone`.

**Complete writes**
- URL + honesty ticks · Go. Writes itt07-iphone.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `2007` · key `itt07-iphone`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-iphone` — do not write `itt06-*` or `itt08-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Street View leftover → `years/2007/sites/streetview/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 284 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt07-amz-c` · sites/amz/c.html

**Open:** `/years/2007/sites/amz/c.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="amz-c"])`
**Need:** field ≥2 (placeholder “2007 leftover”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `google` · `youtube` · `facebook`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="amz-c"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt07-amz-c']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt07-iphone` stays empty. `itt06-amz-c` and `itt08-amz-c` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “2007 leftover”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="amz-c"]`.
- `localStorage['itt07-amz-c']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2007", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt07-amz-c`.
- Does **not** write `itt07-iphone`.
- **Next:** Next leftover → `/years/2007/sites/amz/index.html`
